## Assumptions

- Prototype links navigate between native Flutter screens. Unspecified backend operations remain local previews.
- The OS draws its status and home indicator bars. The app shell draws shared navigation.
- Fixed illustrations retain their source geometry within a responsive, scrollable mobile column.

## REQUEST

- Review generated exact colors and typography for promotion to shared tokens.
- Review source-design duplicates and typos before changing visible content.
